# Research Ledger

## Open frontier questions

- [ ] Can Evans-style ECFG component, tail, cycle, or local-orbit features improve public selector stability for relation-generation surfaces without using the secret scalar?
- [ ] Can any graph-derived feature predict useful public-factor or quadratic-root surfaces on held-out targets after full cost charging?
- [ ] Is there a representation whose reverse-edge structure is cheaper than generic target search, unlike the direct `k -> x(kB)` ECFG?
- [ ] Can the fixed-base many-target ECFG reverse index be combined with relation-surface batching so that the precomputation is less generic than a full scalar table?
- [ ] Can ECFG prefix-hit timing be predicted from public graph-shape features, so target-prefix sparse scans are less generic than ordinary batch precomputation?
- [ ] Does the pre-registered compressed-depth selector keep its rho-beating edge on larger freshly generated Evans-model targets and non-LMFDB curve families?
- [ ] Can graph features help on non-uniform target streams produced by relation surfaces or public filters, where the uniform-target selector barrier no longer applies directly?
- [ ] Which representation or source-construction change can create direct-source certificates touching remaining columns `[6,7]` before adding them to the strict `[14,15]` packet, instead of replaying column 15?

| ECFG-062 | A hybrid first-success route can close the remaining `840_847` p10 gap by combining factor-position timing with cheaper public root work. | HYPOTHESIS | Algorithm Designer | Root-work lower bounds close the gap (ECFG-P110), naive per-surface construction fails (ECFG-N073), `840_847` route rows upgrade to shared-support kernels (ECFG-P111), frozen `848_855`/`856_863` route promotion fails full coverage (ECFG-N074/N076), completion-aware gating preserves below-rho fired kernels (ECFG-P115), and singleton completion cannot be fixed by existing QR policies because selected root-pair supply is zero (ECFG-N077). Root-pair supply augmentation gave two concrete rematerialization work orders (ECFG-P117), but the first added-leaf rematerialization failed (ECFG-N078). Surface-transition sibling generation recovers relation-bearing siblings with public selected-leaf-capacity ordering (ECFG-P118), broad replay shows the term-support/max-leaf ordering lifts verified edge choice across all materialized banks (ECFG-P122), budgeted replay refines this to a route-leaf rule that beats same-route uniform p90 at budget 32 (ECFG-P127), and charged streaming shows a route-leaf-first/uniform-tail hybrid beats pure uniform mean work while still above rho (ECFG-P131/ECFG-N081). Public abstention gates alone fail all-fired rho accounting (ECFG-N082), a frozen public route-success predictor fails validation/live transfer (ECFG-N083), and simple seed switching does not create a live sub-rho stream (ECFG-N084). The cache-join bridge now gives a model-bound positive: route-leaf reaches live cache-compatible exact row pairs at `0.60833577x` rho under replacement-cache accounting, but additive accounting is still `1.50011679x` and the unconditioned all-live denominator is `36.06864972x` (ECFG-P139). The integrated same-event gate improves the live/fresh cache generator by abstaining from false materialized pairs and no-gate route tails, but route-leaf itself is not the best seed under that gate (ECFG-P142/ECFG-N085). The public same-recovered-zero proxy now removes materialized-event eligibility and preserves the fresh abstention signal, but optimistic strict public-screen cost and red-team gate-specific strict cost are still above rho (ECFG-P146/ECFG-N086). A source-shape/factor-index rule gives a sub-rho selector-stage lower bound, but source-root-scan/public-QR charging remains above rho (ECFG-P147/ECFG-N087). Exact signature amortization does not create enough source-root-scan reuse, though a high-risk factor-bucket collapse exposes route-node-only margin (ECFG-P148/ECFG-N088). Exact linear factor-fingerprint equality now explains same-event compatibility without recovered-root multiplicity inside the source-shape/public-zero stream, but current public QR rows do not carry the fingerprint and unique-group costs remain above rho (ECFG-P149/ECFG-N089). The public fingerprint extension rules out greedy calibration selection; the post-hoc max-index-or-gap candidate failed post-975 validation, while fresh `976_983` produced a same-fingerprint cache scalar group and a stricter `gap_ge_6` zero-false selector+cache candidate (ECFG-N090/N091, ECFG-P151/P152). Fresh `984_991` did not promote the wide-gap rule: it produced a same-fingerprint scalar group, but event shape differed and cache repair stayed above rho (ECFG-N092); `gap_ge_6` remains zero-false through `991` but gains no new support (ECFG-P153). Fresh `992_999` restores a same-fingerprint/same-event cache replay below rho and motivates a posthoc low-degree/shared-zero-leaf public graph rule; broad equal factor-QR work fails with false fires, and source/public controls for the refined rule remain above rho (ECFG-P154/P155, ECFG-N093). Fresh `1000_1007` independently replicates same-fingerprint/same-event cache replay below rho and motivates a broader posthoc event-family selector with zero false fires through `1007`, but strict source/public controls remain above rho (ECFG-P156/P157). Frozen `1008_1015` validation adds no fresh support: the source harvester has `0` below-rho cases, Sage/public QR expose `0` surfaces, materialization/cache replay have `0` groups, and the event-family rule adds no fires despite `10` extra fresh route nodes (ECFG-N094). The next positive route is to change support supply or native selected-factor fingerprint/event-shape construction before row-local root/event work, then validate on `1016_1023` or the wider `1008_1023` window. |

| ID | Ruled out | Scope | Evidence | Reusable lesson |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NR-1078 | The current P1071 expanded selector/top-k source-family pool supplies no remaining-column `[6,7]` direction after the strict P1072/P1073 `[14,15]` packet. | Toy p231 target `22050.cf1@11731`, order `11779`, direct-source certificate model, strict baseline P1059 anchor plus P1067 train plus P1069 promotion plus P1072 stage-1 carrier plus P1073 strict column-15 carrier. | `tasks/ecdlp_index_calculus/low_term_total2_p1078_p231_remaining_column_source_family_pivot.py`, `ecdlp_index_calculus_state/experiment_contract_p1078_p231_remaining_column_source_family_pivot.md`, and `ecdlp_index_calculus_state/low_term_total2_p1078_p231_remaining_column_source_family_pivot_probe.json`. Strict scan: `1268` candidate cases, `40` selector/top-k families, `0` single remaining-column hits, `0` family remaining-column hits, full-pool marginal rank gain `0`, full-pool removed columns `[]`, strict packet free columns `[6,7]`. Stage1 controls still detect `4` column-15-only replays; P1073 winner replays column 15 at `0.7810219x` rho and P1074 diagnostic carrier at `0.75182482x` rho. | Sorting the same post-P1073 top-k/source-family surface is exhausted for columns `[6,7]`. The next positive question is a representation/source-construction pivot that targets `[6,7]` before packet insertion, not another column-15 replay router. |
| NR-1079 | Splitting existing post-promotion direct-source certificates into exact target-eliminated relation rows does not expose a remaining-column `[6,7]` direction after the strict `[14,15]` packet. | Toy p231 target `22050.cf1@11731`, order `11779`; exact direct-source certificate pool after P1069; strict P1078/P1073 packet; full parent-certificate charge used for strict success. | `tasks/ecdlp_index_calculus/low_term_total2_p1079_p231_remaining_column_residual_row_pivot.py`, `ecdlp_index_calculus_state/experiment_contract_p1079_p231_remaining_column_residual_row_pivot.md`, and `ecdlp_index_calculus_state/low_term_total2_p1079_p231_remaining_column_residual_row_pivot_probe.json`. The exact-row model aligns with P1078 strict free columns `[6,7]` and rank gain `5`. After excluding strict-packet certificates, the scan covers `35` direct-source certificates and `82` target-eliminated rows. Six rows touch remaining columns, all column `6`, all avoid column `15`, and all are rowspace-dependent: `0` row-level and `0` parent-certificate remaining-column hits. | The obstruction is not just selected-case overaggregation. Existing direct certificates contain only dependent column-6 residual motifs and no column-7 residual supply. P1080 should generate new residual support or change representation, not split or sort this certificate pool again. |

Full graph construction is enumeration, not a shortcut. |

## Positive signals

| ID | Signal | Parameter regime | Evidence | Next validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| P1085 | The P1083 verified column-7 relation integrates into the order-`11779` packet and removes priority column `7` from the free set. | Toy p231 target `22050.cf1@11731`; order-filtered strict packet plus actual verified column-7 relation; Pollard-rho baseline `137` generic steps. | `tasks/ecdlp_index_calculus/low_term_total2_p1085_p231_column7_packet_integration.py` and `ecdlp_index_calculus_state/low_term_total2_p1085_p231_column7_packet_integration_probe.json`. Packet rank moves `14->15`, augmented rank stays `15`, free columns move `[6,7,16]->[6,16]`, parent charge is `0.78832117x` rho, and target descent remains consistent. | Treat P1085 as the current packet baseline. The next validation is P1087: force actual order-`11779` column-6 exported forms and rescore them against the P1085 packet. |
| ECFG-P001 | The constructed ECFGs exactly match the expected binary indegree shape. | Tested targets `22050.cf1@11731`, `67.a1@9803`, `22050.cf1@5641`, `23232.cr1@9643`, `21175.bc1@8089`. | All five graphs have indegree histogram `{0: (N+1)/2, 2: (N+1)/2}` and zero binary violations. | Use graph features as public covariates for relation-surface selection. |
| ECFG-P002 | The ECFG reverse index beats independent rho after batching many targets on the same fixed base. | Nine Evans-model targets, deterministic batches of 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, and 4096 x-ECDLP targets. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: 9/9 targets have amortized wins; best charged speedup is 28.55402678x at batch 4096. | Compare this against ordinary full point lookup, then test graph-specific compression or partial-index variants. |
| ECFG-P003 | Partial component indexing gives a memory-saving fixed-base hybrid win. | Nine targets, deterministic batches up to 4096 and component caps from 2 to 16384. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: 9/9 targets have component-capped wins. Smallest-memory win stores 3.392226% of reverse vertices on `22050.cf1@5641` at cap 256, batch 4096, ratio 0.98132196x independent rho. | Replace oracle component-size caps with a public component selector and compare against generic fixed-base table compression. |
| ECFG-P004 | Public depth-to-cycle indexing beats the component-cap memory frontier on the expanded fixed-base batch test. | Nine targets, deterministic batches up to 4096 and depth caps from 0 to 256. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: 9/9 targets have depth-limited wins. Smallest-memory win stores 2.095911% of reverse parent edges on `22050.cf1@8641` at depth cap 1, batch 4096, ratio 0.9953634x independent rho. | Replicate the depth frontier on fresh random seeds and non-campaign targets. |
| ECFG-P005 | One parent representative plus negation halves the depth-limited reverse-index memory. | Nine targets and depth-index sweep through batch 4096. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: 9/9 targets preserve the depth-limited wins under negation compression. Smallest-memory win stores 1.047955% representative-parent entries on `22050.cf1@8641`, depth cap 1, batch 4096, ratio 0.9953634x independent rho; compression factor is 2.0. | Test whether the shallow-depth selected targets remain above-uniform under independent seed sweeps. |
| ECFG-P006 | Target-prefix sparse scanning gives a smaller extra-memory win than the compressed depth frontier, even after target-filter memory is reported. | Nine targets, deterministic batches up to 4096, prefix caps plus exact half-representative caps. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: 9/9 targets have target-prefix sparse-scan wins. Smallest extra-memory win stores 1 representative parent, 0.00849% of order, on `22050.cf1@11731` at prefix 96, batch 16, ratio 0.98220803x independent rho; total working memory including target filter is 0.144325% of order. | Red-team against generic multi-target batch scanning and vary the batch seed. |
| ECFG-P007 | Half-representative prefix scanning improves the full x-coordinate reverse scan speed in the many-target model. | Nine targets, batch 4096. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: exact half-prefix rows recover all 4096 targets on all nine curves; best speedup is 39.85171508x versus independent rho on `22050.cf1@11731`, improving over the full reverse-index best of 28.55402678x. | Formalize as a restricted x-coordinate theorem and compare to standard negation-map table methods. |
| ECFG-P008 | Compressed depth selection shows above-uniform target density while still beating rho. | Nine-target expanded run. | `functional_graph_probe.json`: winning compressed-depth row on `114224.v1@10453`, depth cap 16, batch 2048, indexes 582 raw parent edges but hits 135 targets versus 113.388 expected uniformly, lift 1.19060084, ratio 0.97489704x independent rho. | Run independent seed sweeps to test whether this lift is stable or a deterministic-batch fluctuation. |
| ECFG-P009 | The compressed-depth graph-shape signal survives independent target-batch seeds. | Five deterministic seed sweep over the same nine targets, batches 64 through 4096. | `functional_graph_seed_sweep.json`: all five seeds have 9 ok targets and rho-beating compressed-depth rows. Best memory frontier is 0.811672% representative entries at depth cap 2, batch 4096, ratio 0.99850562x rho; max winning lift rows range from 1.1291624x to 1.29325307x over uniform parent-edge sampling. | Pre-register the depth/cycle selector and run on fresh targets not chosen from campaign successes. |
| ECFG-P010 | Fresh non-campaign targets preserve pre-registered compressed-depth wins. | Deterministic discovery excluded campaign successes and the prior 9 graph targets, then selected 9 fresh Evans-model targets from primes 5000-12000. | `functional_graph_fresh_preregistered_probe.json`: discovered `23232.cr1@5449`, `21175.bc1@5081`, `21175.bc1@10723`, `21175.bc1@10789`, `114224.v1@7577`, `67.a1@11117`, `23232.cr1@8293`, `114224.v1@6701`, `67.a1@10847`; pre-registered compressed-depth wins on 9/9 targets, best memory 1.103983%, max winning lift 1.30936697x, best speedup 1.09056199x. | Add a pre-registered uniform-memory baseline and increase target count/order. |

## Baselines

| Algorithm | Family | Time | Memory | Notes |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Pollard rho | Generic ECDLP | `sqrt(pi*N/2)` | small | Primary one-target baseline. |
| Full ECFG reverse index | Evans functional graph | `N` graph edges | `N` reverse edges | Exact x-preimage lookup after construction; loses one-shot to rho on tested targets. |
| Amortized ECFG reverse index | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP | `N + O(m)` for `m` targets | `N` reverse edges | Beats `m` independent rho runs once `m` crosses the measured break-even; this is an amortized fixed-base result, not a single-target break. |
| Partial ECFG component index | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `N + fallback*rho + O(indexed)` | capped reverse edges | Keeps rho wins with lower memory on tested batches; still pays a full graph scan and falls back to rho for unindexed components. |
| Depth-limited ECFG index | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `N + fallback*rho + O(shallow indexed)` | shallow reverse edges | Public graph-shape policy that improves the smallest observed memory fraction versus component caps; still pays a full graph scan. |
| Negation-compressed depth index | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `N + fallback*rho + O(shallow indexed)` | one representative parent per indexed image | Uses `x(kB)=x(-kB)` to halve selected reverse-edge memory while preserving recovery and charged work. |
| Pre-registered compressed-depth selector | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `N + fallback*rho + O(shallow indexed)` | graph-only budgeted representative parents | Chooses a depth cap from public graph shape and memory budget before target-hit evaluation; fresh-target runs show rho-beating rows, but still require full graph scan and many targets. |
| Uniform parent-edge control | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `N + fallback*rho + O(sampled indexed)` | deterministic budgeted representative parents | Chooses image vertices by deterministic hash at the same memory budgets; dominates the current depth selector on the 18-target uniform-batch red-team run. |
| Relation RHS ECFG selector | Relation-stream diagnostic | graph construction plus selector membership | selected scalar vertices | Tests whether public ECFG features enrich scalar-side relation-event RHS streams versus deterministic uniform vertex controls; diagnostic only until tied to relation-yield routing. |
| Image-indegree ECFG leaf scheduler | Relation-route leaf scheduler | selected preassociation leaf scans on fixed public routes | selected image vertices | Uses ECFG nodes with positive indegree as the selected leaf set; current diverse-bank toy evidence shows modest aggregate relation-event and event-density lift versus same-size uniform controls, not a full ECDLP speedup. |
| Depth-ge-64 ECFG leaf scheduler | Relation-route leaf scheduler | selected preassociation leaf scans on fixed public routes | selected deep image vertices | Uses ECFG nodes with depth to cycle at least 64 as the selected leaf set; current 61-route pair-bank toy evidence shows aggregate relation-event and event-density lift versus same-size uniform controls, not a full ECDLP speedup. |
| Depth-le-8 ECFG leaf scheduler | Relation-route leaf scheduler | selected preassociation leaf scans on fixed public routes | selected shallow image vertices | Uses ECFG nodes with depth to cycle at most 8 as the selected leaf set; current late fresh surface-pair toy evidence shows aggregate event-density and public-dependency lift versus same-size uniform controls, not a full ECDLP speedup. |
| Target-prefix sparse scan | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP hybrid | `L + fallback*rho + O(hits)` | hit representatives plus public batch target filter | Streams only an ECFG scalar prefix against the public batch target set; useful tiny-memory wins are possible but this is not a one-target attack. |
| Half-representative x-scan | Fixed-base many-target x-ECDLP | `N/2 + O(m)` for `m` x-targets | up to one representative per distinct batch x-target | Scanning one scalar from each `{k, -k}` pair covers every x-coordinate preimage up to sign; beats the full ECFG reverse-index scan in the x-only batch model. |
| Target forward-orbit parent search | Evans functional graph | up to rho per target | small | Finds a parent only when the parent lies on the target vertex's forward orbit; observed rate 7/320. |

| ECDLP functional graphs | Evans, "The Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithm and Functional Graphs" (2011) | ECFG statistics may reveal non-random structure in x-ECDLP graphs. | Needs a target inversion route or selector link; global graph statistics alone do not recover `k`. |
